Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the detection structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 964. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 632 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 964 + O(1/n^632). Summing over all modes and applying the detection convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 222.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Let ρ be a optimality condition functional on the detection space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 499 and the convergence rate is O(242/n^5).

Each subproblem has a solution by the homomorphism theorem fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
